Resident Evil Outbreak File #2 (2004)

100% completion of the selected original PlayStation 2 route, including preserved online features, spoiler-free · 27 areas · 873 tracked items

This page shows everything, including exact locations. It exists so you can search for one specific collectible and find it. If you are playing blind, use the interactive spoiler-free version instead — it hides locations until you ask for them, one item at a time.

Open the spoiler-free interactive guide

Selected Route, Training, and Preserved Online

You are here when: The PS2 title screen, Training Ground, memory-card interface, Network Play, and a private lobby.

Establish a clean, reproducible original-release route before collecting anything.

Use the Original PlayStation 2 Route

Setup

HintOne console release contains the complete selected route.
WhereUse an original File #2 disc on a PlayStation 2 or a tested PS2 emulator.
Full detailConfirm the disc region, controller, real-time menu, memory-card slot, and save operation before measuring progress. There is no secondary or definitive edition to merge.

Begin on a Clean Memory Card

Archive Safety

HintImported data can make rewards appear before their requirements are met.
WhereCreate a blank File #2 save and retain an untouched copy.
Full detailBoot the chosen disc with a new card, verify the Collection and Scenario Select pages contain only initial entries, then clone the card. Keep separate working copies for difficulties, event marks, SP items, online play, and shop purchases.

Complete Training Ground

Tutorial

HintThe practice route teaches systems added for this entry.
WhereChoose Training Ground and follow every on-screen lesson through its exit.
Full detailRead each prompt, exchange an item with the AI partner, command that partner to use an item, help the downed partner stand, recover an item from another inventory, break the blocked door, and reach the tutorial finish.

Preserve the Japanese Network Route

Online Setup

HintCommunity-hosted multiplayer depends on one regional build.
WhereUse a lawfully owned Japanese disc with the current OBSRV connection instructions.
Full detailDump your own Japanese disc and console firmware, preserve untouched hashes, then configure either native PS2 networking or a compatible PCSX2 build. Apply an optional translation patch only to a duplicate image.

Reach an OBSRV Lobby

Online Setup

HintThe retired official network has a maintained archival replacement.
WhereRegister with OBSRV, use its current network values, and enter a private lobby.
Full detailFollow OBSRV's live setup page because addresses may change. Create a separate account, test DNS and memory-card persistence, enter Network Play, create a passworded room, and reconnect once after a cold boot.

Finish a Four-Player Scenario

Online Feature

HintFull cooperation exposes interactions that solo partners cannot demonstrate reliably.
WhereClear any scenario with four human players connected from start to result screen.
Full detailUse a private group, divide inventory and key-item duties, exchange one object, raise an injured teammate, respond with the built-in call system, and keep all four clients connected until the clear is recorded.

Record the Online Infected-State Feature (missable)

Online Feature

HintA failed online survivor can briefly remain interactive.
WhereOn a disposable private-room save, allow one consenting player's virus gauge to fill.
Full detailRemove rare items, leave at least one teammate active, let the chosen gauge reach its limit, and record the temporary player-controlled hostile state. End the test, discard the altered card, and restore the clean online copy.

Unlock and Complete Hard

Mode

HintA progression milestone exposes another ruleset or challenge sequence.
WhereMeet the Easy and Normal clears requirement and reserve 2000 points in Collection points.
Full detailClear all five scenarios on both Easy and Normal, spend 2,000 points, then finish the complete scenario set on Hard.

Unlock and Complete Very Hard

Mode

HintA progression milestone exposes another ruleset or challenge sequence.
WhereMeet the Hard clears requirement and reserve 3000 points in Collection points.
Full detailAfter the Hard set is complete, spend 3,000 points and clear all five scenarios on Very Hard without Infinity.

Unlock and Complete AIPC Amount Set

Mode

HintA progression milestone exposes another ruleset or challenge sequence.
WhereMeet the Easy and Normal clears requirement and reserve 5000 points in Collection points.
Full detailClear all five scenarios on Easy and Normal, spend 5,000 points, then start a run and verify the selectable AI-partner count changes.

Unlock and Complete Infinity

Mode

HintA progression milestone exposes another ruleset or challenge sequence.
WhereMeet the Very Hard clears requirement and reserve 50000 points in Collection points.
Full detailClear all five scenarios on Very Hard, spend 50,000 points, then confirm ammunition and weapon durability no longer decrease.

Unlock and Complete Nightmare

Mode

HintA progression milestone exposes another ruleset or challenge sequence.
WhereMeet the Very Hard clears requirement and reserve 10000 points in Collection points.
Full detailClear all five scenarios on Very Hard, spend 10,000 points, enable Nightmare, and complete a scenario under its altered damage rules.

Unlock and Complete Elimination 1

Mode

HintA progression milestone exposes another ruleset or challenge sequence.
WhereMeet the first two scenarios requirement and reserve 3000 points in Collection points.
Full detailClear Wild Things and Underbelly, spend 3,000 points, then defeat every required target in Elimination 1 and reach its result screen.

Unlock and Complete Elimination 2

Mode

HintA progression milestone exposes another ruleset or challenge sequence.
WhereMeet the first four scenarios requirement and reserve 5000 points in Collection points.
Full detailClear the first four main scenarios, spend 5,000 points, then complete Elimination 2 with ordinary supplies.

Unlock and Complete Elimination 3

Mode

HintA progression milestone exposes another ruleset or challenge sequence.
WhereMeet the earlier Elimination clear requirement and reserve 10000 points in Collection points.
Full detailFinish Elimination 1 or Elimination 2, spend 10,000 points, and clear the combined Elimination sequence.

Unlock and Complete Showdown 1

Mode

HintA progression milestone exposes another ruleset or challenge sequence.

Full detailClear the first three main scenarios, spend 3,000 points, and complete Showdown 1 without importing File #1 data.

Unlock and Complete Showdown 2

Mode

HintA progression milestone exposes another ruleset or challenge sequence.
WhereMeet the five scenarios or event requirement and reserve 5000 points in Collection points.
Full detailClear all five main scenarios, spend 5,000 points, and finish Showdown 2. The alternate event-based gate is not needed on this clean route.

Unlock and Complete Showdown 3

Mode

HintA progression milestone exposes another ruleset or challenge sequence.
WhereMeet the earlier Showdown clear requirement and reserve 10000 points in Collection points.
Full detailFinish Showdown 1 or Showdown 2, spend 10,000 points, and clear the combined Showdown sequence.

Clear Every Scenario on Easy

Difficulty

HintThe lowest setting has its own SP-item placements and completion marks.
WhereFinish all five main scenarios on Easy.
Full detailUse one survivor per run, collect no more than eight planned SP items, trigger any compatible checklist events, and save each result. Confirm five Easy records before moving to Normal.

Clear Every Scenario on Normal

Difficulty

HintNormal supplies a second fixed SP-item layout.
WhereFinish all five main scenarios on Normal.
Full detailRepeat the scenario set on Normal, collect its exclusive SP objects, use alternate exits where available, and verify five Normal records. These clears combine with Easy to expose several Collection extras.

Final 100% Audit

You are here when: Scenario records, event percentages, SP logs, Collection pages, online evidence, and memory-card copies.

Do not declare completion until every independent total agrees.

Collect All 260 SP Items

Completion Audit

HintFive scenario pools and eight survivor pools form one hidden-object total.
WhereVerify 100 scenario-wide SP objects and 160 survivor-specific SP objects.
Full detailCheck every SP page: each main scenario must show twenty objects, and each of the eight survivors must show twenty. The combined count must be 260; this also exposes the final color-themed NPC group.

Reach 100% on All Five Event Lists

Completion Audit

HintMutually exclusive triggers accumulate over many successful exits.
WhereRaise every scenario's event checklist to 100%.
Full detailCompare the five numbered ledgers in this pack against the game after every result screen. Verify 100% separately for End of the Road and Flashback, then for Wild Things, Desperate Times, and Underbelly.

Finish the Four-Difficulty Scenario Grid

Completion Audit

HintEach difficulty changes resources and SP placement.
WhereRecord twenty main clears: five scenarios on Easy, Normal, Hard, and Very Hard.
Full detailMaintain a five-by-four grid outside the game, fill one cell only after its result saves, and keep Infinity disabled. Confirm every cell plus all eleven Extra-mode purchase and clear records.

Finish the Forty-Clear Survivor Grid

Completion Audit

HintCharacter-linked art and costumes require broad scenario coverage.
WhereClear all five scenarios with each of the eight main survivors.
Full detailTrack Kevin, Mark, Jim, George, David, Alyssa, Yoko, and Cindy against all five scenarios. Every one of the forty cells must have a saved result; use this clean matrix instead of importing File #1 completion data.

Purchase the Entire 375-Entry Collection

Completion Audit

HintThe final archive spans images, costumes, movies, sounds, and extra modes.
WhereBuy 145 Gallery, 59 Costume, 146 Movie, 14 Sound, and 11 Extra entries.
Full detailCount purchased markers by page and confirm 145 + 59 + 146 + 14 + 11 = 375. Open every gallery image and movie, preview every costume, play every sound, and launch every Extra option at least once.

Preserve a Verified 100% Card

Completion Record

HintA clean final artifact should prove its own unlock route.
WhereCapture all event, SP, difficulty, survivor, Collection, and online summary pages.
Full detailCold-boot the original disc with the completed card, verify every total, clone the card twice, hash all three copies, and write-protect one archive. Keep the OBSRV profile and unmodified disc image beside—but separate from—the completion save.
